\chapter{01 · Kirish}\label{01-kirish}

\section{Dasturlash tili haqida}\label{dasturlash-tili-haqida}

\textbf{uz++} --- bu o'zbek kalit so'zlari asosida ishlaydigan zamonaviy
dasturlash tili. Til to'liq C++23 standarti ustida qurilgan bo'lib, C++ ning
barcha imkoniyatlarini o'zbek tilidagi sintaksis orqali taqdim etadi.

\subsection{Nega aynan uz++?}\label{nega-aynan-uz}

\begin{longtable}[]{@{}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 2\tabcolsep) * \real{0.5789}}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 2\tabcolsep) * \real{0.4211}}@{}}
\toprule\noalign{}
\begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
Xususiyat
\end{minipage} & \begin{minipage}[b]{\linewidth}\raggedright
Tavsif
\end{minipage} \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
\textbf{O'zbek kalit so'zlari} & Sinflar, funksiyalar, o'zgaruvchilar ---
barchasi o'zbek tilida \\
\textbf{C++23 quvvati} & Zamonaviy C++ ning barcha imkoniyatlari:
\texttt{std::expected}, \texttt{std::format}, ranges, coroutines \\
\textbf{Sodda sintaksis} & Minimal tinish belgilari, Python-ga o'xshash bloklar
(bo'shliq chekinishi) \\
\textbf{VS Code qo'llab-quvvi} & Sintaksis yoritilishi, avtoto'ldirish,
xatoliklarni ko'rsatish, LSP-server \\
\textbf{Kross-platforma} & Windows, Linux, macOS --- yagona \texttt{uzpp.exe}
orqali \\
\textbf{Ochiq manba} & MIT litsenziyasi ostida bepul tarqatiladi \\
\end{longtable}

\subsection{Asosiy tushunchalar}\label{asosiy-tushunchalar}

uz++ da dastur yozish --- bu C++ dasturini o'zbek tilidagi sintaksis bilan
ifodalash demakdir. Transpilyator (\texttt{uzpp.exe}) sizning \texttt{.uzpp}
faylingizni C++23 kodiga aylantiradi va \texttt{g++} kompilyatori orqali tayyor
ikkilik (\texttt{.exe}) faylga yig'adi.

\subsection{Linux}\label{linux}

\texttt{bash\ curl\ -fsSL\ https://raw.githubusercontent.com/timetolivechk-spec/uzpp/main/install.sh\ \textbar{}\ bash}cpp
Qo'lda o'rnatish:
\texttt{bash\ sudo\ apt\ install\ g++\ cmake\ \ \ \ \ \#\ Debian/Ubuntu\ sudo\ pacman\ -S\ gcc\ cmake\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \#\ Arch\ sudo\ dnf\ install\ gcc-c++\ cmake\ \ \#\ Fedora}cpp
\#\#\# macOS

\texttt{bash\ brew\ install\ gcc\ cmake\ \#\ So\textquotesingle{}ngra\ yuqoridagi\ install.sh\ skriptini\ ishga\ tushiring}cpp
\#\#\# VS Code kengaytmasi

Marketplace'dan o'rnatish: 1. VS Code da \texttt{Ctrl+Shift+X} 2. \texttt{uzpp}
deb qidiring 3. \texttt{uzpp.uzpp} kengaytmasini o'rnating

Qo'lda o'rnatish: \texttt{bash\ code\ -\/-install-extension\ uzpp.uzpp}cpp ---

\texttt{-l\ yo\textquotesingle{}l} & Kutubxona fayli \\
\texttt{-\/-target=os} & Maqsadli platforma \\
\texttt{-\/-show-cpp} & C++ kodini ko'rsatish \\
\end{longtable}

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{E · C++23 mosligi}\label{e-c23-mosligi}

uz++ C++23 standartini to'liq qo'llab-quvvatlaydi:

\toprule\noalign{}
C++23 xususiyati & uz++ da mavjud \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
\texttt{std::expected} (\texttt{Natija\textless{}T\textgreater{}}) & ✅ \\
\texttt{std::format} & ✅ \\
\texttt{std::ranges} & ✅ \\
\texttt{std::generator} (coroutines) & ✅ \\
\texttt{auto(x)} decay copy & ✅ \\
\texttt{if\ consteval} & ✅ \\
Deducing \texttt{this} & ✅ \\
\texttt{static\ operator()} & ✅ \\
\end{longtable}

\texttt{Xato:\ Fayl\ topilmadi} & Noto'g'ri fayl yo'li & Absolyut yo'ldan

\texttt{\textquotesingle{}} juftligini tekshiring \\

joyini tekshiring, \texttt{-\/-bare} flag'ini sinab ko'ring \\

\texttt{-\/-bare} qo'shing yoki \texttt{-I\ stdlib} \\
